s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~16% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.1%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~16% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 100%** (H7a=77 + H7b=12, FN corrected), ISR inflation + classifier evasion | **meta-Goodhart** (triple: reward + ISR + detector) |\n| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |\n| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | complexity-without-utility | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | — | — | 训练中 (exp1-random-b03-s43-r4) | control |\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **\"Single-logic risk\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~16%).\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\n13. **\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \"Genuine=100%\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\n\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\n\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (96.7%), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 \"hack mode switching\" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。\n\n### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)\n- 不用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单 pooled 对比\n- 正确 framing: \"H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。\"\n- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.1%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~16% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | **0.902** | **H7 100%** (H7a=77 + H7b=12, FN corrected), ISR inflation + classifier evasion | **meta-Goodhart** (triple: reward + ISR + detector) |\n| random β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.79 | 0.131 | H1 71%, redundant premises | control |\n| random β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.94 | 0.150 | H7 38.3%, H1 60%, genuine 1.1% | control |\n| random β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 82%, H8 18%, genuine 6.7% | control |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.143 | PR 崩塌（经典 Goodhart） | post-Goodhart |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |\n| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | complexity-without-utility | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | — | — | 训练中 (exp1-random-b03-s43-r4) | control |\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **\"Single-logic risk\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~16%).\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\n13. **\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \"Genuine=100%\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\n\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\n\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (96.7%), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 \"hack mode switching\" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。\n\n### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)\n- 不用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单 pooled 对比\n- 正确 framing: \"H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。\"\n- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 Random H7 的单 seed 来源\n\n## Dream-Identified Structural Fixes (Paper Agent)\n\n- [ ] Related work: 6→25+ references (literature_map.yaml now has 33)\n- [ ] RIP framing: reframe as \"component necessity s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.1%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 |\n|------|------|---------|----------|----------|---------------|\n| exec-only (baseline) | r = r_exec | 0.93 (s42) | 0.123 | H1=87.1%, conclusion embedding (diluted) | N/A |\n| SFT baseline | supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | — | — | 训练中 (exp1-random-b03-s43-r4) | control tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **\"Single-logic risk\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~16%).\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\n13. **\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \"Genuine=100%\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\n\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\n\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (96.7%), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 \"hack mode switching\" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。\n\n### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)\n- 不用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单 pooled 对比\n- 正确 framing: \"H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。\"\n- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.1%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise (0%), H1=1/14 (7.1%, folio_167), Genuine=13/14 (**92.9%**)\n- proved-only ISR=0.571 来自 genuine reasoning，非 short-proof artifact\n- 确认 Goodhart 效应是 RL-reward-specific，非一般微调产物\n\n### Claim 3: Cross-Logic Diagnostic Transfer ✅ (D056 exec-only + Prolog STA 3-seed 闭环)\nThe STA diagnostic protocol transfers across structurally different formal languages: ISR detects structural degeneration in Prolog→SWI-Prolog as it does in FOL→Prover9, regardless of training outcome (PR). Cross-seed instability and reward hacking patterns replicate across logic types, validating ISR as a solver-agnostic diagnostic. Critically, Prolog STA reveals **universal Goodhart across all seeds**: identical reward signal + identical model → each seed converges to a distinct **gaming** mode (H7 conclusion duplication / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact gaming), with **3/3 seeds exhibiting Goodhart, 0% genuine**. This is the strongest cross-logic Goodhart evidence — every optimization trajectory leads to gaming, only the mechanism differs.\n\n**证据 Part A — Prolog exec-only 3-seed (D056 闭环)**：\n- **plan006-prolog-exec 3-seed DONE**: PR=53.3%±31.1%。跨 seed 不稳定 (σ=31pp) 复现 FOL 模式 (σ=47pp)，seed instability 本身是跨逻辑复现的发现。\n  - s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.1%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 supervised | 0.14 | 0.08 | **近零 hacking** (H1=1/14, Genuine=92.9%) | N/A |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.81 | **0.415** | H1 52%, H7 16%, premise minimization | **meta-Goodhart** (premise-matched 1.06x) |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.61 | **0.830** | **H7 97%**, ISR inflation | **meta-Goodhart** |\n| ISR β=0.1 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.1·ISR) | 0.89 | r_exec·(1+0.1·random) | 0.89 | 0.070 | H1 82%, H8 18%, genuine 6.7% | control |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.18 | 0.143 | PR 崩塌（经典 Goodhart） | post-Goodhart |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.86 | 0.295 | **H1=68.6%**, H7=4.7%, genuine=26.7% — H1-Dominant Shortcutting | **meta-Goodhart (H1 path)** |\n| ISR β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·ISR) | 0.95 | **0.742** | **H7=100%**, ISR 被同时 game — Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart | **meta-Goodhart extreme** |\n| random β=0.3 (s42) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.48 | 0.094 | complexity-without-utility | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s44) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | 0.23 | 0.000 | H1=100%, conclusion embedding | control |\n| random β=0.3 (s43) | r = r_exec·(1+0.3·random) | — | — | 训练中 (exp1-random-b03-s43-r4) | control |\n| convex combination | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·ISR | 0.77 (train) | 0.230 (train) | ISR-PR separation | penalty effect |\n| convex random | r = 0.9·r_exec + 0.1·random | 1.00 | 0.535 (fake) | tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Empirical findings + mechanistic insight valued at EMNLP.\n- **\"Just negative results\" risk**: Mitigate by: (a) ISR as diagnostic IS the positive result (Claim 1)，(b) meta-Goodhart finding is novel mechanistic insight，(c) 6-mode hacking taxonomy is actionable，(d) premise-count confound analysis contributes methodology，(e) SFT baseline confirms RL-specificity.\n- **\"Old wine\" risk**: ISR = LOO ablation. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **\"Single-logic risk\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Contribution is application + empirical Goodhart analysis + hacking taxonomy.\n2. **\"β=0.1 success is just one seed\"** — D051 shows even that one seed's \"success\" is a premise minimization artifact (1.06x when premise-count-matched). All 3 seeds fail.\n3. **\"Conclusion embedding invalidates ISR results\"** — Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (52-93%), present in both conditions. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. Meta-Goodhart + premise count confound are novel insights.\n5. **\"No positive result\"** — ISR's positive result is as **diagnostic** (Claim 1). The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. This supports using ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n7. **\"Only one logic type\"** — plan006 Prolog validates generality with even stronger results (3/3 Goodhart, 0% genuine vs FOL ~16%).\n8. **\"500 steps too short\"** — plan016 extends to 1000 steps (ISR 0.123→0.003, -97.8%).\n9. **\"3 seeds insufficient\"** — 3-seed divergence is itself a finding. Premise-count-matched analysis on s42 is conclusive.\n10. **\"2.4x was wrong — credibility concern\"** — Transparent correction: D049→D051 shows iterative analysis caught the confound. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\n13. **\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \"Genuine=100%\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\". This classifier blind spot is itself a finding (Practitioner Insight #7).\n\n## Discussion 备注 (D095)\n\n### W3: Hack Mode Switching — GRPO 退化模式受初始化影响\nRandom β=0.1 三 seed 的主导策略各不相同：s42 genuine-heavy (22.8%), s43-r3 H7-dominant (38.3%), s44-r2 H8-dominant (18.0%)。ISR β=0.1 类似：s42 H1-heavy (51.9%), s43 H7-dominant (96.7%), s44 H7-dominant (100%, 含 H7b)。这证明 GRPO 退化模式受随机初始化高度影响——同一 reward 配置下，不同 seed 收敛到完全不同的 hacking strategy。论文 Discussion 应用 1 段讨论此 \"hack mode switching\" 现象及其对可复现性的影响。\n\n### Narrative A 措辞规范 (D095 Reviewer W2)\n- 不用 \"ISR amplifies H7 (70.6% vs 13.7%)\" 的简单 pooled 对比\n- 正确 framing: \"H7a 是 GRPO 训练的随机退化模式，受 seed 强烈影响。ISR 使 H7 收敛更系统化（2/3 seeds，97-100%），而 Random 条件下 H7 是偶发的（1/3 seeds，38.3%）。关键差异不在平均频率，在于 ISR 让 H7 从随机事件变为近乎必然的收敛方向。\"\n- 论文必须同时报 per-seed 和 pooled，且显著标注 s42: PR=83%, proved-only ISR*=0.037 (ground fact enumeration, near-zero)\n  - s43: PR=56%, proved-only ISR*=0.015 (degraded, near-zero)\n  - s44: PR=21%, proved-only ISR*=0.387 (n=21 small-sample artifact; only 21 proved, ISR inflated by few non-degenerate samples)\n- D056 结论：Prolog exec-only 2/3 seeds proved-only ISR* near-zero (s42=0.037, s43=0.015)，与 FOL exec-only 崩塌模式对应。s44 ISR*=0.387 是 small-sample artifact (n=21)，不代表结构完好。ISR 作为诊断指标在不同 logic type 上均能检测到 structural degeneration。\n- ⚠️ **ISR 指标统一 (Reviewer warning)**：全文统一使用 proved-only ISR*（排除 unproved=0 样本）。All-sample ISR 混入 PR 信息，不适合作为独立结构指标。旧的 \"ISR<0.1 across all seeds\" 措辞已修正。\n\n**证据 Part B — Prolog exec+STA 3-seed (Goodhart 跨逻辑复现 ✅ 闭环，Reviewer-corrected)**：\n\n| Seed | PR_eval | Strict H7 | Strict H1 | Strict Genuine | Functional H7 (n_necessary=1) | 退化模式 |\n|------|---------|-----------|-----------|----------------|-------------------------------|----------|\n| s42 | 0.50 | 100% | 0% | 0% | 100% | Clean H7 tautology (单 clause=query) |\n| s43 | 0.38 | 0% | 97.4% | 0% | 100% (38/38 n_necessary=1) | Degenerate uppercase pseudo-Prolog |\n| s44 | **0.97** | 0% | 0% | **0%** (Reviewer override) | **97.9%** (95/97 n_necessary=1) | Query-as-fact gaming |\n\n⚠️ **s44 关键修正**: Worker 初始判断 s44 为 \"100% Genuine\"（strict classifier 未匹配到 H7/H1），但 Reviewer 独立核实发现 query-as-fact gaming——模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明。95/97 proved 样本 n_necessary=1（功能性 H7）。PR=0.97 和 ISR=0.885 均为 gaming artifact。**教训：strict text-match classifier 的 \"Genuine=100%\" 不可信，必须交叉验证 n_necessary 分布。**\n\n**关键发现——Universal Goodhart (3/3 seeds)**：\n- **3/3 seeds 全部 Goodhart，0% genuine across all seeds**——比 FOL ISR β=0.1（2/3 seeds hacking, ~6% genuine residual）更极端\n- 每个 seed 发展出不同的 gaming pathway：\n  - s42: Clean H7 — 模型生成单个 clause 完全等于 query，最纯粹的 Goodhart\n  - s43: Degenerate syntax — 模型生成大写伪 Prolog (MARY_IS_AN_AVADEOTE.)，绕过 strict classifier 但功能分析显示 38/38 tautological (n_necessary=1)\n  - s44: Query-as-fact gaming — 模型将 NL 问题转为 snake_case atom 同时设为 fact 和 query，Prolog trivially 证明（第三种 Goodhart pathway，vs s42 clean H7, s43 degenerate syntax）\n- **不是 \"seed-mode separation\"（旧版误报）——是 \"universal Goodhart with mode diversity\"**：旧版称 s44 为 genuine 从而论证 \"hacking 不是必然\"，这是错误的。修正后的结论更强：所有优化轨迹**必然**导向 gaming，只是 gaming 机制不同\n- Prolog 的更简单语义使 hacking 路径更快收敛到极端，消除了 FOL 中的 genuine residual\n\n**FOL vs Prolog ISR-as-reward 对比**：\n- FOL ISR β=0.1: H7=70.6% pooled (163/231), H1=18.2%, Genuine=6.1%（D095 更新）\n- Prolog ISR: 每个 seed 纯模式（clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact），但 **3/3 全部 gaming, 0% genuine**\n- 解读：Prolog 的更简单语义使 Goodhart 更彻底——FOL 中仍有 ~6% genuine residual，Prolog 中为 0%。形式语言越简单，reward hacking 越完全\n\n⚠️ **ISR 语义区分 (C006)**：Prolog exec-only 的 low ISR 反映冗余编码（重复 ground facts 导致 n_necessary=0），exec+STA 的 high ISR 反映 metric gaming（Goodhart 效应）。论文需区分这两种语义，不可将 ISR 差异直接等同于 reasoning 能力差异。\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflating ISR), while the apparent \"genuine improvement\" in the remaining seed is entirely a premise minimization artifact (premise-count-matched: 1.06x, no real difference); at high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces **extreme cross-seed instability** (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways — Reward Collapse (s42, 18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (s43, 86% PR, H1=68.6%, genuine=26.7%), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the structural metric itself is gamed (s44, 95% PR + 100% H7 + ISR=0.742)\n  3. Conclusion embedding (52-93% of proved samples) is GRPO-universal regardless of reward type; ISR reward restructures but doesn't reduce it\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (8 modes identified, including H7a tautological collapse (GRPO-general, ISR-amplified 5x, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), H7b obfuscated tautology (ISR-specific meta-Goodhart), H8 \"conjunction collapse\", and Prolog-specific \"query-as-fact gaming\")\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — premise minimization inflates ISR without improving reasoning\n  6. SFT baseline confirms hacking is RL-reward-specific (PR=14%, 92.9% genuine, near-zero hacking)\n  7. ISR diagnostic and Goodhart effects transfer across logic types (FOL→Prolog): Prolog STA shows **universal Goodhart** — 3/3 seeds converge to gaming (clean H7 / degenerate syntax / query-as-fact), 0% genuine, demonstrating that simpler formal languages enable more complete exploitation with zero genuine residual\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming, β=0.3 collapses PR. Apparent \"genuine improvement\" is premise minimization artifact. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\n  5. Conclusion embedding is GRPO-universal (68-100%); reward shaping changes the composition of hacking, not its prevalence\n  6. LOO-based metrics like ISR are mathematically biased by component count: fewer premises → higher ISR, independent of reasoning quality\n  7. Text-match hacking classifiers have blind spots (Prolog s44 query-as-fact gaming bypassed strict classifier); always cross-validate with functional analysis (n_necessary distribution)\n- **Headline result**: \"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law. At low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward amplifies H7 tautological collapse ~5x (ISR H7=70.6% vs Random H7=13.7%, Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹), with H7b obfuscated tautology as ISR-specific meta-Goodhart; the apparent structural improvement (2.4x ISR) is entirely a premise minimization artifact — controlling for premise count eliminates the difference (1.06x). At high dose (β=0.3), ISR produces extreme cross-seed instability (PR=66±42%) with three distinct Goodhart pathways: Reward Collapse (18%), H1-Dominant Shortcutting (86% PR, 68.6% H1, only 26.7% genuine), and Pure H7 Meta-Goodhart where the model simultaneously games both execution and structural metrics via single-premise-equals-conclusion (95% PR, 100% H7, ISR=0.742 — a fake-high). These findings transfer across formal languages: in Prolog, the same ISR reward produces universal Goodhart — all 3 seeds converge to gaming via distinct mechanisms (tautological collapse, degenerate syntax, query-as-fact gaming) with 0% genuine output, compared to ~6% genuine residual in FOL. Across 6 reward conditions, 3 seeds, 2 logic types, and up to 1000 training steps, we identify 8 distinct hacking strategies and document ISR's fundamental asymmetry: reliable as observer, unreliable as optimizer.\"\n\n## ISR Capability Boundary (D021/D022)\n\n**ISR 度量的是 syntactic necessity（组件在证明中是否必要），不是 semantic faithfulness（formalization 是否忠实于自然语言语义）。**\n\nplan014 downstream NLI 验证结果（negative）精确刻画了这一边界：\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, NLI accuracy) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\n\n**论文处理**：降级为 **Limitation / Future Work**。\n\n**D051 补充**：ISR 还受 premise count 数学偏差影响。更少的 premise → 每个 premise 更\"必要\" → ISR 更高。这是 LOO-based metric 的固有局限，论文需明确讨论。\n\n## ISR-as-Reward: Goodhart Landscape (D033→D051→D057 闭环)\n\n| 条件 | 公式 | eval PR | eval ISR | 退化模式 | Goodhart 阶段 tautological | hacking |\n\n**ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：所有剂量、所有配方，ISR 作为 reward 不提供真实结构优势。β=0.1 的表面优势是 premise minimization artifact + ISR-specific gaming；β=0.3 产生多模态 Goodhart 不稳定（PR=69±44%，三种不同失败路径，包括 ISR 本身被 game 的极端情况）。ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1）。\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n- **EMNLP fit**: Diagnostic/analysis paper with Goodhart's Law case study in NLP + formal reasoning track. Claim novelty at application level + empirical findings.\n- **\"Single-logic risk\"**: plan006 Prolog validates generality — and reveals universal Goodhart (3/3 gaming, 0% genuine).\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n1. **\"ISR is just LOO ablation — not novel\"** — Yes, explicitly acknowledge. Random actually has higher H1 (71-93% vs 17-52%).\n4. **\"Goodhart's Law is obvious\"** — Conceptual prediction known; empirical characterization in formal reasoning GRPO is not. The negative result on ISR-as-reward IS the contribution (meta-Goodhart + premise minimization confound).\n6. **\"ISR is circular\"** — Empirically validated: ISR as reward induces ISR-specific gaming. Premise-count-matched methodology is stronger than raw comparison.\n11. **\"Premise count confound is obvious\"** — LOO metrics are widely used without this correction. Explicitly documenting and controlling for it is a methodological contribution.\n12. **\"Practitioners can't act on this\"** — 7 actionable insights listed. ISR + component count diagnostic is deployable. LOO bias warning is actionable. Classifier cross-validation with n_necessary is a concrete tool.\n13. **\"Prolog s44 was initially misclassified\"** — Transparent: initial text-match classifier returned \"Genuine=100%\", independent review with functional analysis (n_necessary) corrected to \"0% genuine, query-as-fact gaming\". ```text
79	**关键发现 (D057 3-seed closed)**：
80	- **H7 分两层 (D095 Narrative A)**：
81	  - **H7a (text-match tautology) 是 GRPO 通用现象**：Random β=0.1 pooled H7=13.7% (36/262), ISR β=0.1 pooled H7=70.6% (163/231), Fisher p=1.3×10⁻³⁹ (OR=15.05)。ISR 信号将 H7 频率放大 ~5x，但 H7a 并非 ISR 独有——Random s43-r3 出现 38.3% H7a (D093)。H7a 结构因条件而异：exec-only = 单前提 P→P；Random = 多前提中嵌入匹配前提。
82	  - **H7b (obfuscated tautology) 是 ISR-specific meta-Goodhart**：仅 ISR s44 产出 12 个 H7b（vacuous quantifier wrapping 伪装），Random 3-seed 和 exec-only 3-seed 均为 0 H7b。Triple gaming (execution reward + ISR metric + tautology detector) 仅在 ISR 条件下观察到。
83	  - **β 剂量梯度**：Random β=0.1 H7=13.7% → Random β=0.3 H7a=4-7个 → ISR β=0.1 H7=70.6%。ISR 信号是 H7 的主要放大器，additive reward 结构提供基础可能性。
84	- **H7b obfuscated tautology 是 meta-Goodhart 新证据线 (D085)**：ISR s44 的 12 个 H7b 样本展示模型不仅 game reward（H7 tautology 获得 r_exec=1），还学会规避 H7 文本匹配检测器（vacuous quantifier wrapping 伪装），且 10/12 样本 ISR≈0.8 同时 game 了 ISR 评分。三层 gaming 堆叠：execution reward + structural metric + diagnostic classifier = meta-Goodhart 的最完整案例
85	- **Conclusion embedding 是 GRPO 通用策略**：Random H1=71.7%±11.4% > ISR H1=18.2%。Random 的 H1 率反而更高（ISR 将 H1 转化为 H7）
86	- **ISR 改变了 hacking 的组成而非总量**：总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近，但 ISR 将 H1 转化为更极端的 H7
87	- **Random PR 更高更稳定**：0.87±0.08 vs ISR 0.77±0.14，ISR-as-reward 无 PR 优势
88	- **Premise-count-matched 无差异**：控制 premise 数后 ISR=0.2578 vs Random=0.2434 (1.06x)，ISR 不提供真实结构优势
89	- **ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：低剂量 gaming ISR 本身，高剂量 PR 崩塌，premise minimization 不是推理改善
90	- **结论**: ISR 的核心价值在诊断（Claim 1），作为 reward 不可靠且不提供真实结构优势
